Direct optimization:
- Choose n_opt = 1024 (or 2048). We'll optimize sequence p (nonnegative sum=1). Objective = 2n * max(C) (since sum=1). Using FFT for C.
- Use soft-max: S_soft = T * logsumexp(C/T). We minimize S_soft. Gradient wrt a as derived. For sum constraint, use gradient g = dS_soft/da - mean(dS_soft) (to keep sum), then update a ← a - η*g. Project to ≥0, renormalize to sum=1.
- Temperature T: Since C values scale ~1/n ~0.001. We can define T = temp_scale * max(C) maybe dynamic. For soft max to approximate max, T should be small relative to differences. Use T = 0.1 * (max(C)-median?) or T = 0.1/n? We can experiment. For gradient, if T too small, gradient roughly exact max gradient (only bin argmax), which can oscillate; soft helps. Use T = 0.05 * max(C) initially, decrease to 0.01*max(C). Or use adaptive softmax with top bins.
- Learning rate: scale η relative to a values (~1/n). Since C gradient has units? S_soft ~1/n, gradient ~? Need choose η ~ n? We can use normalized a values O(1) by setting sum=n (so a_i ~1). Then C~n, S_soft~n, gradient O(1). Objective =2n*max(C)/n². For sum=n, easier. Let a_i ~1. Update with η ~0.1. Let's use sum target N = n (so mean 1). Then C values around 1..n. Implement FFT convolution helper:
def conv_full_fft(x, y):
    n = len(x)+len(y)-1
    size = 1 << (n-1).bit_length()
    return np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(x, size) * np.fft.rfft(y, size), size)[:n]
For C: conv_full_fft(a,a).
For grad: H=conv_full_fft(p,a)[:n]; U=conv_full_fft(p,a[::-1])[n-1:n-1+n] (check length). p length 2n-1, a_rev length n, conv length 3n-2; slice indices n-1 to 2n-2 inclusive length n. Good.
Test with small mentally: a=[a0,a1], p=[p0,p1,p2]. U_0=sum p_k a_{k} valid k 0,1: p0a0+p1a1. conv(p,a_rev) with a_rev=[a1,a0], conv index n-1=1: p0*a_rev1 + p1*a_rev0 = p0a0+p1a1 yes. U_1=sum p_k a_{k-1}: k=1 p1a0 + k=2 p2a1. conv index2: p0*a_rev2(0)+p1*a_rev1(a0)+p2*a_rev0(a1)=p1a0+p2a1. Good.
Then dm_da = U + H.

- Fast continuous optimization using polynomial and Fourier bases, multiple params, to get candidate continuous densities.

Let's detail fast_score:
def fast_score(a):
    n=a.size; S=a.sum(); if S<1e-12 return inf
    pad=1 << (2*n-1).bit_length()
    A=np.fft.rfft(a, pad)
    C=np.fft.irfft(A*A, pad)[:2*n-1].real
    C=np.maximum(C,0) # remove roundoff
    return 2.0*n*C.max()/(S*S)
But L-BFGS objective should be smooth; use soft_score:
def soft_score_raw(a, Tparam):
   S=a.sum(); C...; T=max(1e-12, Tparam*max(C.max()-C.mean(), C.max()*1e-4, 1e-6))
   C_scaled=C/T; shift=C_scaled.max(); msoft=T*(shift+np.log(np.sum(np.exp(C_scaled-shift))))
   return 2*n*msoft/(S*S)
No gradient. For exact best, use fast_score.

def make_param_objective(basis, N, T):
   def func(theta):
      s=theta[0]; c=theta[1:]
      raw = basis @ c (where basis shape NxD)
      raw -= raw.mean()
      f = 1.0 + s*raw
      if np.min(f) < -1e-9:
          # penalty but still use clipped for score to keep finite
          f_pos = np.maximum(f, 0.0)
      else...
      S=f_pos.sum()
      if S<N*0.1: return 1e100
      f_pos = f_pos / S * N  # mean1
      C=fft conv
      Msoft = ...
      score=2*N*Msoft/(S**2? after normalization S=N, so 2*N*Msoft/N² = 2*Msoft/N) . Since f_pos normalized mean1, S=N. Use exact formula.
      penalty = 0.0; if minf<0: penalty = 1e5 * float(np.sum(np.minimum(f,0)**2)) + 1e3*minf? Use large.
      return score + penalty
   return func

Parameter objective:
def param_soft_objective(theta, basis, N, T):
   v = basis @ theta
   # subtract mean to avoid large constant? Since softmax invariant, do v -= np.mean(v) for numerical.
   v = np.clip(v, -50, 50)
   vmax = np.max(v); z = np.sum(np.exp(v-vmax)); a = np.exp(v-vmax)/z * N
   # score soft
   C = fft_convolve_real(a,a) # or directly rfft
   if C.max()<=0 return 0
   if T is None: T = max(1.0, 0.02*N)
   # Use C values maybe sum fixed N².
   scaled = C / T; s = np.max(scaled); msoft = T*(s + np.log(np.sum(np.exp(scaled-s))))
   return 2.0 * N * msoft / (N*N) = 2*msoft/N.

Let's write pseudo-code:

import numpy as np, time, math
from scipy.optimize import minimize

def next_pow2(m):
    n=1
    while n<m: n <<=1
    return n

def conv_fft(x,y):
    need = len(x)+len(y)-1
    size = next_pow2(need)
    fx = np.fft.rfft(x, size)
    fy = np.fft.rfft(y, size)
    return np.fft.irfft(fx*fy, size)[:need].real

def build_basis(N, K=10):
    x=(np.arange(N)+0.5)/N
    cols=[]
    y=2*x-1
    # poly p=1..5
    for p in range(1,6):
       b=y**p
       b=b-b.mean()
       if np.all(np.abs(b)<1e-12): continue
       b=b/(np.sqrt(np.mean(b*b))+1e-12)
       cols.append(b)
    for k in range(1,K+1):
       ang=2*np.pi*k*x
       for b in (np.sin(ang), np.cos(ang)):
          b=b-b.mean()
          if np.all(np.abs(b)<1e-12): continue
          b=b/(np.sqrt(np.mean(b*b))+1e-12)
          cols.append(b)
    return np.column_stack(cols)

def softmax_from_theta(theta, basis, N):
    v = basis.dot(theta)
    v -= np.mean(v)
    v = np.clip(v, -50, 50)
    vmax = v.max()
    e = np.exp(v - vmax)
    a = e / e.sum() * N
    return a

def fast_score(a):
    n=a.size
    S=a.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(S) or S < 1e-12: return np.inf
    need=2*n-1
    size=next_pow2(need)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    C=np.fft.irfft(A*A, size)[:need].real
    C=np.maximum(C,0.0)
    return float(2.0*n*C.max()/(S*S))

def soft_score(a, T=None):
   n=a.size; S=a.sum()...
   C...
   if T is None: T=max(1.0,0.02*n)
   scaled=C/T; sms=scaled.max(); msoft=T*(sms+np.log(np.sum(np.exp(scaled-sms))))
   return float(2.0*n*msoft/(S*S))

def objective_theta(theta, basis, N, T):
   a=softmax_from_theta(theta,basis,N)
   return soft_score(a,T)

def score_candidate(a):
   # scale to max1000 and official
   if a.size==0: return np.inf, None
   a=np.asarray(a,float); a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
   S=a.sum()
   if S<0.01: return np.inf, None
   m=a.max()
   if m>0: a=a*(1000.0/m)  # official clamps anyway
   a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
   # maybe if S after scale? scale*old S, min? if old S huge? fine
   sc=float(evaluate_sequence(a.tolist()))
   return sc, a

But official evaluate_sequence itself clamps to 1000; scaling by max before okay. However if a.max() very small? a mean1 => max≥1. Good.

For fast tracking, use fast_score(a) with a mean1; objective scale invariant, no need scale.

Fitting theta:
def fit_theta(a,basis):
   a=np.maximum(a,1e-6); v=np.log(a); v-=v.mean()
   theta,_,_,_=np.linalg.lstsq(basis, v, rcond=None)
   return np.clip(theta, -6, 6)

Direct polish:
def polish_sequence(a, deadline, max_iters? ):
   n; a=a.copy(); a=np.maximum(a,0); S=a.sum(); if S<... reset uniform; a=a/S*n
   best=a.copy(); best_sc=fast_score(a)
   m,v... current=a
   rng=np.random.default_rng(1234)
   it=0
   while time.monotonic() < deadline:
      C=conv_fft(current,current)
      T=max(1.0,0.02*n)
      scaled=C/T; sh=scaled.max(); p=np.exp(scaled-sh); p/=p.sum()
      # gradient
      H=conv_fft(p,current)[:n]
      U=conv_fft(p,current[::-1])[n-1:n-1+n]
      dm=H+U
      g=dm - np.mean(dm)
      it+=1
      m=0.9*m+0.1*g; v=0.999*v+0.001*g*g
      mh=m/(1-0.9**it); vh=v/(1-0.999**it)
      a_new=current - 0.05 * mh/(np.sqrt(vh)+1e-8)
      # occasional random
      if it % 200 ==0:
          a_new = a_new * np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.02,n));
      a_new=np.maximum(a_new,0)
      S=a_new.sum()
      if S < 0.1*n:
          a_new=a + 0.02*rng.normal(0,1,n); a_new=np.maximum(a_new,0)
      a_new=a_new/S*n if S>0 else uniform
      sc=fast_score(a_new)
      if np.isfinite(sc):
          current=a_new
          if sc < best_sc - 1e-10:
              best=a_new; best_sc=sc
          elif sc > 5*best_sc + 0.5:
              current=best; # reset maybe
      else: current=best
   return best,best_sc

```python
"""
Find a non-negative coefficient sequence minimizing
    2 * n * max(convolve(a, a)) / (sum(a))**2.

Strategy:
1. Use a smooth low-dimensional log-domain parameterization:
       a_i = exp(v_i) / sum_j exp(v_j) * n
   where v is a linear combination of a small set of smooth basis functions.
2. Optimize the parameters with L-BFGS-B using a smoothed max objective.
3. Keep the best exact-score candidate.
4. If time remains, polish the best sequence with a projected gradient/Adam
   step in the full coefficient space.
"""

import time
import math
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize


def next_power_of_two(m):
    """Smallest power of two >= m."""
    p = 1
    while p < int(m):
        p <<= 1
    return p


def conv_fft(x, y):
    """Real full linear convolution using FFT."""
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64)
    need = len(x) + len(y) - 1
    size = next_power_of_two(need)
    fx = np.fft.rfft(x, size)
    fy = np.fft.rfft(y, size)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fx * fy, size)[:need]
    return c.real


def fast_score(a):
    """Fast score using FFT convolution. Returns inf for invalid input."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-12:
        return np.inf
    need = 2 * n - 1
    size = next_power_of_two(need)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, size)[:need].real
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    mx = float(np.max(c))
    if not math.isfinite(mx):
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * mx / (s * s))


def soft_score(a, t=None):
    """Smoothed version of fast_score for optimization."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-12:
        return np.inf
    need = 2 * n - 1
    size = next_power_of_two(need)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, size)[:need].real
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)

    if t is None:
        t = max(1.0, 0.015 * n)
    t = float(max(1e-9, t))

    scaled = c / t
    sh = float(np.max(scaled))
    e = np.exp(scaled - sh)
    m_soft = t * (sh + float(np.log(np.sum(e))))
    if not math.isfinite(m_soft):
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * m_soft / (s * s))


def make_basis(n, fourier_k):
    """Smooth basis functions for log-domain parameters."""
    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    y = 2.0 * x - 1.0
    cols = []

    # Low-degree polynomial features.
    for p in range(1, 6):
        b = y ** p
        b = b - np.mean(b)
        sd = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(b * b)))
        if sd > 1e-12:
            b = b / sd
            cols.append(b)

    # Fourier features.  Sin terms are antisymmetric under x -> 1-x;
    # cos terms allow more general smooth shapes.
    for k in range(1, int(fourier_k) + 1):
        ang = 2.0 * math.pi * k * x
        for b in (np.sin(ang), np.cos(ang)):
            b = b - np.mean(b)
            sd = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(b * b)))
            if sd > 1e-12:
                b = b / sd
                cols.append(b)

    if not cols:
        cols.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))
    return np.column_stack(cols)


def sequence_from_theta(theta, basis, n):
    """Normalize exp(Phi theta) to mean 1."""
    v = basis.dot(theta)
    v = v - float(np.mean(v))
    v = np.clip(v, -50.0, 50.0)
    vmax = float(np.max(v))
    e = np.exp(v - vmax)
    a = e / float(np.sum(e)) * float(n)
    return a


def theta_objective(theta, basis, n, t):
    """Objective for scipy.optimize.minimize."""
    a = sequence_from_theta(theta, basis, n)
    return soft_score(a, t)


def fit_theta_to_sequence(a, basis):
    """Least-squares fit of log(a) to the basis."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-7)
    v = np.log(a)
    v = v - float(np.mean(v))
    theta, _, _, _ = np.linalg.lstsq(basis, v, rcond=None)
    return np.clip(theta, -6.0, 6.0)


def official_score_from_mean_one(a):
    """Scale to max 1000 and score with the provided evaluator."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if a.size == 0 or s < 0.01 or not math.isfinite(s):
        return np.inf
    mx = float(np.max(a))
    if mx > 0.0:
        a = a * (1000.0 / mx)
        a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    val = evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in a])
    return float(val)


def make_simple_sequences(n, rng):
    """Deterministic/handmade smooth starting sequences, mean 1."""
    seqs = []
    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    y = 2.0 * x - 1.0

    # Asymmetric polynomial/ramp family.
    for s in (0.0, 0.25, 0.45, 0.60, 0.68, 0.75, 0.85, 0.95, 1.0):
        a = 1.0 + float(s) * y
        a = np.maximum(a, 1e-7)
        a = a / float(np.mean(a))
        seqs.append(a)

    # Cubic/quintic antisymmetric shapes.
    for c3 in (-0.8, -0.4, 0.0, 0.4, 0.8):
        for c5 in (-0.5, 0.0, 0.5):
            a = 1.0 + 0.7 * y + float(c3) * y ** 3 + float(c5) * y ** 5
            a = np.maximum(a, 1e-7)
            a = a / float(np.mean(a))
            seqs.append(a)

    # Antisymmetric sine combinations.
    for s1 in (0.6, 0.8, 1.0):
        for s2 in (-0.7, -0.3, 0.0, 0.3, 0.7):
            for s3 in (-0.3, 0.0, 0.3):
                a = (1.0
                     + float(s1) * np.sin(2.0 * math.pi * x)
                     + float(s2) * np.sin(4.0 * math.pi * x)
                     + float(s3) * np.sin(6.0 * math.pi * x))
                a = np.maximum(a, 1e-7)
                a = a / float(np.mean(a))
                seqs.append(a)

    return seqs


def make_random_sequences(basis, n, rng, count):
    """Random log-domain sequences, mean 1."""
    seqs = []
    m = basis.shape[1]
    for _ in range(int(count)):
        theta = rng.normal(0.0, 0.7, size=m)
        # Bias slightly toward low frequency by scaling down high indices is optional.
        a = sequence_from_theta(theta, basis, n)
        seqs.append(a)
    return seqs


def make_default_sequence(n=4096):
    """A safe asymmetric ramp fallback, scaled for the evaluator."""
    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    y = 2.0 * x - 1.0
    a = 1.0 + 0.69 * y
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-7)
    a = a / float(np.mean(a))
    mx = float(np.max(a))
    if mx > 0.0:
        a = a * (1000.0 / mx)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return [float(v) for v in a]


def polish_sequence(a, deadline):
    """Full-sequence projected gradient polish, mean fixed to length."""
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0 or time.monotonic() >= deadline:
        return None, np.inf

    rng = np.random.default_rng(20240527)
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-10)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(s):
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(n)
    a = a / s * float(n)

    current = a.copy()
    best = a.copy()
    current_score = fast_score(current)
    best_score = current_score

    beta1 = 0.90
    beta2 = 0.999
    lr = 0.06
    m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    step = 0
    no_improve = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        # Compute convolution and soft-max weights.
        t = max(1.0, 0.018 * n)
        c = conv_fft(current, current)
        scaled = c / t
        sh = float(np.max(scaled))
        p = np.exp(scaled - sh)
        p /= float(np.sum(p))

        # Gradient of soft-max convolution wrt current.
        # dC_k/da_j = a_{k-j} + a_{j-k}, with out-of-range terms zero.
        h = conv_fft(p, current)[:n]
        u = conv_fft(p, current[::-1])[n - 1:n - 1 + n]
        grad_c = h + u
        g = grad_c - float(np.mean(grad_c))

        step += 1
        m = beta1 * m + (1.0 - beta1) * g
        v = beta2 * v + (1.0 - beta2) * (g * g)
        mh = m / (1.0 - beta1 ** float(step))
        vh = v / (1.0 - beta2 ** float(step))

        # Multiplicative update on log-current, clipped for stability.
        u_dir = mh / (np.sqrt(vh) + 1e-8)
        u_dir = np.clip(u_dir, -6.0, 6.0)
        new_a = current * np.exp(-lr * u_dir)

        # Occasional small random shock to escape local minima.
        if step % 500 == 0:
            shock = np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.015, size=n))
            new_a = new_a * shock

        new_a = np.maximum(new_a, 0.0)
        ns = float(np.sum(new_a))
        if ns <= 1e-8 or not math.isfinite(ns):
            new_a = best + 0.01 * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, size=n)
            new_a = np.maximum(new_a, 0.0)
            ns = float(np.sum(new_a))
            if ns <= 1e-8 or not math.isfinite(ns):
                new_a = best.copy()
                ns = float(np.sum(best))
        new_a = new_a / ns * float(n)

        sc = fast_score(new_a)

        # Reset if catastrophic.
        if not math.isfinite(sc) or sc > 10.0 * max(1.0, best_score) + 2.0:
            new_a = best.copy()
            sc = best_score

        current = new_a

        if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-10:
            best = current.copy()
            best_score = sc
            no_improve = 0
            if best_score < 2.0:
                print(f"polish improved to {best_score:.8f} at step {step}", flush=True)
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        if no_improve > 20000:
            # Refresh from best with a small perturbation.
            current = best * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.03, size=n))
            cs = float(np.sum(current))
            if cs > 0.0:
                current = current / cs * float(n)
            else:
                current = best.copy()
            no_improve = 0

    return best, best_score


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800, **kwargs):
    """
    Main search function.
    Returns the best sequence found before the deadline.
    """
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    budget_s = max(0.0, float(budget_s))
    overall_deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, budget_s - 10.0)

    # Length choice.  4096 is large enough for a good continuous limit and
    # still fast for FFT-based scoring and optimization.
    n = 4096
    basis = make_basis(n, fourier_k=12)
    m_params = basis.shape[1]
    bounds = [(-6.0, 6.0)] * m_params
    t_soft = max(1.0, 0.015 * n)

    best_sequence = None
    best_official = np.inf
    best_fast = np.inf

    def update_best_with_sequence(a):
        nonlocal best_sequence, best_official, best_fast
        if a is None:
            return
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
        sc_fast = fast_score(a)
        if not math.isfinite(sc_fast):
            return
        if best_sequence is None or sc_fast < best_fast + 1e-8:
            best_fast = sc_fast
            off = official_score_from_mean_one(a)
            if math.isfinite(off) and off < best_official:
                best_official = off
                best_sequence = a.copy()
                print(f"new best official score = {best_official:.8f} "
                      f"fast={sc_fast:.8f}", flush=True)

    # Build candidate pool.
    simple = make_simple_sequences(n, rng)
    randoms = make_random_sequences(basis, n, rng, 160)

    simple_scored = []
    for idx, a in enumerate(simple):
        sc = fast_score(a)
        if math.isfinite(sc):
            simple_scored.append((float(sc), idx, a))

    random_scored = []
    for idx, a in enumerate(randoms):
        sc = fast_score(a)
        if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < 2.35:
            random_scored.append((float(sc), idx, a))

    simple_scored.sort()
    random_scored.sort()

    starts = []
    # Always keep the best deterministic starts.
    for sc, idx, a in simple_scored[:18]:
        starts.append(fit_theta_to_sequence(a, basis))

    # Keep the best random smooth starts.
    for sc, idx, a in random_scored[:32]:
        starts.append(fit_theta_to_sequence(a, basis))

    # A few raw random thetas to preserve diversity.
    for _ in range(12):
        starts.append(np.clip(rng.normal(0.0, 0.8, size=m_params), -6.0, 6.0))

    if not starts:
        starts = [fit_theta_to_sequence(make_default_sequence(n), basis)]

    # Evaluate initial candidates officially.
    for a in [x for x in [item[2] for item in simple_scored[:8]] if a is not None]:
        update_best_with_sequence(a)
    for a in [x for x in [item[2] for item in random_scored[:8]] if a is not None]:
        update_best_with_sequence(a)

    # Reserve time for polish and final checks.
    polish_reserve = 300.0 if budget_s > 900.0 else 80.0
    param_deadline = overall_deadline - polish_reserve
    if param_deadline < time.monotonic() + 30.0:
        param_deadline = overall_deadline - 20.0

    results = []

    # First optimization pass: moderate budget over many starts.
    for idx, theta0 in enumerate(starts[:42]):
        if time.monotonic() >= param_deadline:
            break
        theta0 = np.asarray(theta0, dtype=np.float64)
        maxfun = 1200
        try:
            res = minimize(
                theta_objective,
                theta0,
                args=(basis, n, t_soft),
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                bounds=bounds,
                options={
                    "maxiter": 80,
                    "maxfun": maxfun,
                    "ftol": 1e-8,
                    "gtol": 1e-6,
                    "eps": 1e-5,
                }
            )
        except Exception:
            continue

        a = sequence_from_theta(res.x, basis, n)
        sc = fast_score(a)
        if math.isfinite(sc):
            results.append((float(sc), res.x.copy()))
            update_best_with_sequence(a)
        if idx % 8 == 0:
            print(f"start {idx:03d}: fast={sc:.8f} time_left={param_deadline-time.monotonic():.1f}s",
                  flush=True)

    # Second pass: larger budget for the most promising parameters.
    results.sort()
    best_params = [res_theta for _, res_theta in results[:10]]
    for idx0, theta0 in enumerate(best_params):
        if time.monotonic() >= overall_deadline - 60.0:
            break
        theta0 = np.asarray(theta0, dtype=np.float64)
        remaining = max(0.0, overall_deadline - 120.0 - time.monotonic())
        maxfun = int(min(8000.0, max(2000.0, remaining * 10.0)))
        try:
            res = minimize(
                theta_objective,
                theta0,
                args=(basis, n, t_soft),
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                bounds=bounds,
                options={
                    "maxiter": 200,
                    "maxfun": maxfun,
                    "ftol": 1e-9,
                    "gtol": 1e-7,
                    "eps": 1e-6,
                }
            )
        except Exception:
            continue

        a = sequence_from_theta(res.x, basis, n)
        update_best_with_sequence(a)
        if idx0 % 3 == 0:
            print(f"refine {idx0:02d} done", flush=True)

    # If nothing valid was found, use a safe fallback.
    if best_sequence is None:
        def_seq = make_default_sequence(n)
        # Convert to mean-one array for possible polish.
        arr = np.asarray(def_seq, dtype=np.float64)
        mx = float(np.max(arr))
        if mx > 0.0:
            arr = arr / mx  # now max 1
        s = float(np.sum(arr))
        if s > 0.0:
            best_sequence = arr / s * float(n)
        else:
            best_sequence = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        best_official = official_score_from_mean_one(best_sequence)
        best_fast = fast_score(best_sequence)
        print("using fallback sequence", best_official, flush=True)

    # Polish if there is enough remaining time.
    if time.monotonic() < overall_deadline - 30.0:
        polish_end = min(overall_deadline, time.monotonic() + min(300.0, overall_deadline - time.monotonic()))
        polished, polished_score = polish_sequence(best_sequence, polish_end)
        if polished is not None and math.isfinite(polished_score):
            update_best_with_sequence(polished)

    # Final scale to the evaluator's preferred range.
    out = np.asarray(best_sequence, dtype=np.float64)
    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)
    mx = float(np.max(out))
    if mx > 0.0:
        out = out * (1000.0 / mx)
        out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)

    # If for some reason sum is too small, bump scale up.
    s = float(np.sum(out))
    if s < 1.0:
        out = out * max(1.0, (10.0 / max(s, 1e-12)))
        out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)

    final_score = float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in out]))
    print("final returned score:", final_score, flush=True)
    return [float(x) for x in out]
```
